HTML and PHP About The Author Karthigadevi is an Associate Professor and the Department of Computer Science and Information Systems at Anna University.  He earned a M.E degree in computer science from Bharathiyar University and an B.E degree in computer science from the University of Anna. He Was worked in industry as a software engineer and project manager, specializing in Java and various web technologies—HTML, CSS, JavaScript, JavaServer Pages, and servlets. He has taught a full range of computer science courses, including client-side and server-side web-programming courses. Email : [ebookauthor011@gmail.com](mailto:ebookauthor011@gmail.com)
